# Phase 3: cognition cleanup

## What was there / what it does

The original folder had 41 Python modules, including matrix planning and provider adapters. It owns provider-neutral requests/responses, context projection, engine/provider boundaries, grounded self-state serialization, model residency/routing, tool dispatch, response-quality checks and durable matrix traces.

Production chains checked:

- Application → composition engine factories → rule/test/local LLM/lifecycle/fleet/router implementations. Deterministic test engines/providers remain selectable configured production development workflows; their names do not make them dead.
- Conversation matrix controller → default evaluator registry and package exports → classification, context/privacy/evidence/authority/tool/routing/expression/response planners → validation and trace store. Relative imports through `matrix.__init__` are real reachability, not orphan modules.
- Runtime response → cognitive context/operation → cognitive system → full/compact context assembler → engine → provider. Context's authoritative self-state property constructs the projection directly; serializer reads the canonical core/embodiment/operational sources.
- Ollama adapter → draft naturalization/classification → at most one text-only quality retry → grounded fallback. Retry does not authorize or re-execute tools.
- Runtime/application model lifecycle worker and managed engines → installed/ready checks, begin/end-use accounting, idle sweeping; tray operator methods use install/unload/status for explicit roles.
- Cognitive system → tool dispatcher → capability gateway → host-owned authority. Grounding contract governs evidence trust; it does not create action permission.

## What was wrong / what was split, merged and deleted

The 1,111-line repetition guard combined rejecting drafts with producing replacement requests and fallback text. `quality_repair.py` now owns the latter two functions; AST comparison verified their full definitions remained unchanged after movement. Patterns are shared from one classifier catalog, not copied into another source of truth. Provider and tests import the moved functions from their owner without an obsolete re-export.

The 926-line assembler mixed context selection with evidence rendering. `context_evidence.py` now formats supplied system capability records, nested structured values, continuity, workspace and filesystem evidence. The assembler is 622 lines; tuple/list rendering uses one sequence branch rather than two duplicate branches. It still distinguishes knowledge from permission and presents unknowns explicitly.

Merged the 33-line cognition domain evaluator into its 50-line default registry module; removed `cognition_domain.py` and updated the registry import. Domain evaluation still produces the same contributions.

`AuthoritativeSelfState` is used by `CognitiveContext.authoritative_self_state`, which already instantiated it directly. Deleted its unused factory and 15 property facades that repeated the supplied canonical objects. Tests now construct the same validated projection as production and inspect its supplied fields; existing serialization/assembler tests continue to verify the actual model-facing projection.

Deleted model lifecycle `update_selection`, `background_role`, `note_used`, `ensure_loaded` and `unload_all`. No production or dynamic string caller used them. Configuration changes reconstruct the graph; live managed inference checks `ensure_available` and uses `begin_use/end_use`; explicit operator unload is role-based. Removed the obsolete backend preload method from the lifecycle protocol. Deleted three tests whose only purpose was the unused background-role/preload/aggregate path; idle accounting and missing-model tests now exercise the live methods without weakening their expectations.

## What was fixed

The first-person host access denial matcher expected “I do not have…” and missed “I am an AI model and do not have…”, allowing generic boilerplate to replace an actual unauthorized tool result. It now recognizes bounded intervening first-person text. The existing tool-result regression verifies a retry reports the specific capability status.

Specific short emotion repetition now takes precedence over generic reflexive handoff rejection. The existing repeated-emotion regression verifies that diagnosis; handoff checks remain active for otherwise unrepeated drafts. Both pre-existing response-quality baseline failures are repaired.

## Canonical ownership / what remains

No new state store, authority source or provider graph was introduced. Request/response data remain provider-neutral; engine and provider ABCs represent distinct boundaries. Matrix records are derived plans/traces, not permission grants. Privacy projection remains bound to authenticated principals. Grounding hierarchy remains separate from host authority. Model residency accounting remains one manager over configured roles and observed backend state.

Small planner modules stay separate where they enforce distinct privacy, authority, evidence and exposure contracts. Read-only `EvidenceMatrix.missing_required` remains an explicit diagnostic projection. CognitiveSystem's proposal/approval APIs remain reviewed action authority boundaries, not automatically invoked model capabilities; no live frontend caller is claimed. Both substantial assemblers remain active: compact conversation projection and full/tool-facing assembly serve different contexts rather than duplicate persistent state.

No live Ollama integration was made available; live-provider failures remain observable and are not skipped.

## Verification

- Targeted provider quality, lifecycle, self-state, matrix, routing and production-role tests: **224 passed**.
- Final cognition/context/quality/matrix/production/runtime/memory/import gate after evidence extraction: **403 passed**.
- Compile and whitespace checks passed. Removed names/modules have no active source/test import/call sites. The changed provider imports and factory ownership were repaired.
- Full suite after the final formatter extraction: **3,598 passed, 8 unchanged remaining baseline failures, 2 skipped** (126.25 seconds). Two previously failing quality regressions are repaired; three fossil tests are removed with their obsolete lifecycle routes. The remaining failures require live Ollama (five) or reflect Windows assumptions on Linux (three).

## Commit

Resolve this report's checkpoint with `git log -1 --format=%H -- docs/development/cognition-cleanup-report.md`. The checkpoint is pushed to `origin/work`; cleanup continues through all phases before merging to main.
